import * as React from "react";
import { Navigate, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useStudentAssignment } from "@/hooks/useStudentAssignments";
import { useUserSettings } from "@/contexts/UserSettingsContext";
import { singleSectionOf } from "@/lib/drillRunSpec";
import { launchDrill } from "@/lib/drillLaunch";
import {
  SECTION_DIRECTIONS,
  formatSectionMinutes,
  sectionMinutes,
} from "@/lib/sectionDirections";
import "./section-directions.css";

/**
 * The screen before an assigned section, laid out the way LawHub lays out its
 * own: a white card on a blue-grey field, the section number, the clock, the
 * question count, the official directions, and one button.
 *
 * Nothing is timed here. The clock starts when GO TO QUESTIONS is pressed,
 * which is also when the assignment becomes "in progress", so a student who
 * opens this and walks away has lost nothing.
 *
 * It takes the whole screen on purpose. The Classroom rail and the list of
 * other work are exactly what a student should not be looking at in the
 * minute before a timed section.
 */
export default function SectionDirectionsPage() {
  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { settings } = useUserSettings();
  const { assignment, loading, error } = useStudentAssignment(assignmentId);

  const section = React.useMemo(
    () => (assignment ? singleSectionOf(assignment.question_qids ?? []) : null),
    [assignment],
  );

  if (loading && !assignment) {
    return (
      <main className="section-directions" aria-busy="true">
        <p className="section-directions-status" role="status">
          Loading your section…
        </p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (error || !assignment || !assignmentId) {
    return (
      <main className="section-directions">
        <div className="section-directions-card">
          <h1 className="section-directions-title">Section not found</h1>
          <p className="section-directions-body">
            This assignment could not be loaded. Open your Classroom and try again from there.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="section-directions-go"
          onClick={() => navigate("/classroom")}
        >
          BACK TO CLASSROOM
        </button>
      </main>
    );
  }

  // Not one section (a mixed set, or a set that was edited after assigning):
  // the ordinary assignment page knows what to do with it.
  if (!section) {
    return <Navigate to={`/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(assignmentId)}`} replace />;
  }

  // Finished work is a record, not a door. Results live in History.
  if (assignment.status === "completed") {
    return <Navigate to="/history" replace />;
  }

  const minutes = sectionMinutes(settings.defaultTimingMode);
  const questionCount = assignment.question_qids.length;
  // Only LR sections are qid-shaped today; an RC assignment will arrive with
  // its own kind on the row rather than being inferred from a qid pattern.
  const directions = SECTION_DIRECTIONS.lr;

  const goToQuestions = () => {
    const timerMode =
      settings.defaultTimingMode === "35" ? undefined : settings.defaultTimingMode;
    const query = launchDrill({
      kind: "assignment",
      assignmentId,
      timerMode,
      options: { returnTo: "/classroom" },
    });
    navigate(`/drill?${query}`);
  };

  return (
    <main className="section-directions">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="section-directions-back"
        onClick={() => navigate("/classroom")}
      >
        ← Back to home
      </button>
      <div className="section-directions-card">
        <h1 className="section-directions-title">Section {section.section}</h1>
        <p className="section-directions-line">
          {minutes === null
            ? "Untimed section"
            : `Total Section Time - ${formatSectionMinutes(minutes)}`}
        </p>
        <p className="section-directions-line">
          {questionCount} Question{questionCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}
        </p>
        <p className="section-directions-body">
          <strong>Directions:</strong> {directions}
        </p>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="section-directions-go"
        onClick={goToQuestions}
        autoFocus
      >
        GO TO QUESTIONS
      </button>
    </main>
  );
}
----
import type { TimerMode } from '@/types/drill';

/**
 * The page a student sees between "Open the set" and the first question of an
 * assigned PrepTest section: section number, the clock, the question count,
 * and the official directions. Modelled on LawHub's directions screen, because
 * the point of assigning a real section is that it feels like the real thing.
 */

export type SectionKind = 'lr' | 'rc';

export function sectionDirectionsRoute(assignmentId: string): string {
  return `/section/${encodeURIComponent(assignmentId)}`;
}

/** LSAC's own wording, verbatim. Not to be edited for voice. */
export const SECTION_DIRECTIONS: Record<SectionKind, string> = {
  lr:
    'Each question in this section is based on the reasoning presented in a brief passage. ' +
    'In answering the questions, you should not make assumptions that are by commonsense ' +
    'standards implausible, superfluous, or incompatible with the passage. For some questions, ' +
    'more than one of the choices could conceivably answer the question. However, you are to ' +
    'choose the best answer; that is, choose the response that most accurately and completely ' +
    'answers the question.',
  rc:
    'Each set of questions in this section is based on a single passage. The questions are to ' +
    'be answered on the basis of what is stated or implied in the passage. For some questions, ' +
    'more than one of the choices could conceivably answer the question. However, you are to ' +
    'choose the best answer; that is, choose the response that most accurately and completely ' +
    'answers the question.',
};

/**
 * Minutes on the clock for a timing mode, or null when there is no clock.
 * Standard is 35; 52.5 and 70 are the 1.5x and 2x accommodations.
 */
export function sectionMinutes(mode: TimerMode | undefined): number | null {
  switch (mode) {
    case '52.5':
      return 52.5;
    case '70':
      return 70;
    case 'unlimited':
      return null;
    default:
      return 35;
  }
}

/** "35 minutes", "52 minutes 30 seconds", "70 minutes". */
export function formatSectionMinutes(minutes: number): string {
  const whole = Math.floor(minutes);
  const seconds = Math.round((minutes - whole) * 60);
  if (seconds === 0) return `${whole} minutes`;
  return `${whole} minutes ${seconds} seconds`;
}
----
/*
 * The directions screen before an assigned section. Colours are LawHub's, not
 * the app's tokens, on purpose: this screen is meant to look like the test.
 */
.section-directions {
  min-height: 100vh;
  min-height: 100dvh;
  background: #c5d5e3;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 72px 24px 48px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;
  color: #1f1f1f;
}

/* Leaving costs nothing: the clock has not started. Quiet, top-left, out of
   the card so it never reads as part of the test. */
.section-directions-back {
  align-self: flex-start;
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  color: #1f1f1f;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 17px;
  padding: 6px 4px;
  margin: -44px 0 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
}

.section-directions-back:hover,
.section-directions-back:focus-visible {
  color: #b8650b;
  outline: none;
}

.section-directions-card {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 1310px;
  min-height: 430px;
  background: #ffffff;
  border-radius: 18px;
  padding: 18px 84px 48px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  text-align: center;
}

.section-directions-title {
  font-size: 20px;
  font-weight: 400;
  margin: 0;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.section-directions-line {
  font-size: 20px;
  margin: 0;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.section-directions-body {
  text-align: left;
  font-size: 20px;
  line-height: 1.35;
  margin: 44px 0 0;
}

.section-directions-status {
  font-size: 18px;
}

.section-directions-go {
  margin-top: 32px;
  background: #b8650b;
  color: #ffffff;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 14px;
  padding: 18px 20px;
  font-size: 20px;
  font-family: inherit;
  letter-spacing: 0.01em;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.section-directions-go:hover,
.section-directions-go:focus-visible {
  background: #a3590a;
  outline: 3px solid #1f1f1f;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .section-directions {
    padding: 32px 12px 32px;
  }
  .section-directions-card {
    padding: 16px 20px 32px;
    min-height: 0;
  }
  .section-directions-title,
  .section-directions-line,
  .section-directions-body,
  .section-directions-go {
    font-size: 17px;
  }
}

  DialogDescription,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipProvider,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip";

type HighlightMode = "none" | "yellow" | "pink" | "orange" | "underline" | "erase";

export const FONT_SCALES = [
  { key: "sm", label: "Small", value: 0.9 },
  { key: "md", label: "Default", value: 1.0 },
  { key: "lg", label: "Large", value: 1.15 },
  { key: "xl", label: "Extra Large", value: 1.3 },
] as const;

export interface ToolbarDirections {
  title: string;
  body: React.ReactNode;
  /** Screen-reader description of the dialog. */
  srDescription: string;
}

/**
 * The official LR section directions. Kept here as the default so the LR
 * player's call sites stay unchanged; RC passes its own set.
 */
export const LR_DIRECTIONS: ToolbarDirections = {
  title: "Directions",
  srDescription: "Directions for the LSAT Logical Reasoning section.",
  // LSAC's current digital wording, the same text the section's directions
  // page shows. The old paper-test version told students to blacken a space
  // on an answer sheet they do not have.
  body: <p>{SECTION_DIRECTIONS.lr}</p>,
};

interface FontScaleOption {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
}

interface Props {
  highlightMode: HighlightMode;
  setHighlightMode: (mode: HighlightMode) => void;
  /** Pre-formatted timer label, e.g. "20:41". Empty string when no timer. */
  timerLabel: string;
  /** Actual timer mode — drives label prefix exactly. Undefined hides timer box. */
  timerMode?: "countdown" | "stopwatch";
  /** Paint the timer as overrun. A countdown that has passed zero keeps going. */
  timerOverBudget?: boolean;
  /**
   * Fraction of the section's time still left, 0..1. Drives the depleting bar
   * the official interface paints under the clock. Omit for a stopwatch or an
   * untimed section and the bar is not rendered — there is nothing to deplete.
   */
  timerProgress?: number;
  /** Controlled Find Text value. */
  searchTerm: string;
  setSearchTerm: (s: string) => void;
  /** Active font scale value (1.0 = default). */
  fontScale: number;
  setFontScale: (n: number) => void;
  /** Show the notes toggle icon (Smart Drill only). */
  notesButtonVisible?: boolean;
  /** Whether the notes panel is currently open (drives the active state). */
  notesOpen?: boolean;
  onToggleNotes?: () => void;
  /**
   * Directions dialog contents. Defaults to the LR section directions; pass a
   * different set for another section, or `null` to hide the button entirely.
   */
  directions?: ToolbarDirections | null;
  /**
   * Rendered in the left group between Directions and Find Text — the slot RC
   * uses for "Passage Only View", which sits there in the official interface.
   */
  leftTools?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Rendered in the right cluster after the text controls and before the timer.
   * RC puts its find-match readout and the next/previous match buttons here.
   * (This used to claim response masking, a review grid and full screen, none
   * of which RC has ever passed.)
   */
  rightTools?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Hide the underline/marker/eraser group. Only pass false for a surface that
   * has no text to annotate — the tools are part of the official look.
   */
  highlightToolsVisible?: boolean;
  /** Override the font-size options. Defaults to FONT_SCALES. */
  fontScaleOptions?: readonly FontScaleOption[];
  /**
   * Extra controls appended inside the Aa popover beneath a divider. RC puts
   * line height and color scheme here so text settings stay in one control.
   */
  a11yExtraSections?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * LR ⇄ RC section switch shown at the far left of the toolbar. Lets a student
   * jump straight from a Logical Reasoning drill into the RC passages and back.
   */
  sectionToggle?: {
    active: "lr" | "rc";
    onSelect: (section: "lr" | "rc") => void;
  };
}

export default function OfficialDrillToolbar({
  highlightMode,
  setHighlightMode,
  timerLabel,
  timerMode,
  timerOverBudget,
  timerProgress,
  searchTerm,
  setSearchTerm,
  fontScale,
  setFontScale,
  notesButtonVisible,
  notesOpen,
  onToggleNotes,
  directions = LR_DIRECTIONS,
  leftTools,
  rightTools,
  highlightToolsVisible = true,
  fontScaleOptions = FONT_SCALES,
  a11yExtraSections,
  sectionToggle,
}: Props) {
  const [directionsOpen, setDirectionsOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [aaOpen, setAaOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const tools: { mode: HighlightMode; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
    { mode: "underline", label: "Underline", icon: <UnderlineIcon /> },
    { mode: "yellow", label: "Yellow highlighter", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor="#FACC15" /> },
    { mode: "pink", label: "Pink highlighter", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor="#EC4899" /> },
    { mode: "orange", label: "Orange highlighter", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor="#F97316" /> },
    { mode: "erase", label: "Eraser", icon: <EraserIcon /> },
  ];

  const toggle = (mode: HighlightMode) => {
    setHighlightMode(highlightMode === mode ? "none" : mode);
  };

{"command": "cd \"C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch\" && sed -n 160,430p src/components/drill/official/OfficialDrillToolbar.tsx", "description": "Read toolbar render"}

Tool result:
  };

  const timerLabelPrefix =
    timerMode === "countdown"
      ? "Section Time Remaining"
      : timerMode === "stopwatch"
      ? "Time Elapsed"
      : null;
  const paddedTimer = (timerLabel || "00:00").replace(/^(\d):/, "0$1:");
  // The bar is the at-a-glance read; the digits are the precise one. It goes
  // amber in the last quarter and red in the last tenth so the student feels
  // the section closing without having to parse numbers mid-question.
  const timerFraction =
    typeof timerProgress === "number"
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, timerProgress))
      : null;
  const timerBarColor =
    timerOverBudget || (timerFraction !== null && timerFraction <= 0.1)
      ? "bg-red-600"
      : timerFraction !== null && timerFraction <= 0.25
      ? "bg-amber-500"
      : "bg-green-600";

  return (
    <>
      <div className="border-b border-slate-300 bg-slate-50">
        <div className="px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-[14px] text-slate-800">
          {sectionToggle && (
            <div
              role="group"
              aria-label="Section"
              className="mr-1 inline-flex overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white"
            >
              {(["lr", "rc"] as const).map((section) => {
                const isActive = sectionToggle.active === section;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={section}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={isActive}
                    title={section === "lr" ? "Logical Reasoning" : "Reading Comprehension passages"}
                    onClick={() => !isActive && sectionToggle.onSelect(section)}
                    className={cn(
                      "px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors",
                      isActive
                        ? "bg-[#7f1d1d] text-white"
                        : "text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100",
                    )}
                  >
                    {section.toUpperCase()}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          {directions && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setDirectionsOpen(true)}
              className="rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors text-[14px] font-semibold"
            >
              Directions
            </button>
          )}

          {leftTools}

          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Find Text, Type Here"
            aria-label="Find text in passage"
            className="ml-1 rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 h-8 text-[13px] text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 w-[210px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-slate-700"
          />
          {searchTerm && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchTerm("")}
              className="text-[12px] text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 underline"
              aria-label="Clear search"
            >
              clear
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Right cluster: tools | Aa + line-spacing | timer */}
          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {highlightToolsVisible && (
              <>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                  {tools.map((t) => {
                    const isActive = highlightMode === t.mode;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={t.mode}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggle(t.mode)}
                        aria-pressed={isActive}
                        aria-label={t.label}
                        title={t.label}
                        className={cn(
                          "p-1 rounded-sm transition-colors flex items-center justify-center",
                          isActive
                            ? "bg-white border border-slate-700 shadow-[inset_0_2px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]"
                            : "border border-transparent hover:bg-slate-200",
                        )}
                      >
                        {t.icon}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                <PipeSeparator />
              </>
            )}

            <Popover open={aaOpen} onOpenChange={setAaOpen}>
              <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Text size"
                  title="Text size"
                  className={cn(
                    "px-2 py-1 rounded-sm text-slate-900 flex items-baseline transition-colors",
                    aaOpen
                      ? "bg-white border border-slate-700 shadow-[inset_0_2px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]"
                      : "border border-transparent hover:bg-slate-200",
                  )}
                >
                  <span aria-hidden className="text-[20px] font-bold leading-none">A</span>
                  <span aria-hidden className="text-[13px] font-bold leading-none">a</span>
                </button>
              </PopoverTrigger>
              <PopoverContent align="start" sideOffset={6} className="w-60 p-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-lg rounded-md max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto">
                {fontScaleOptions.map((opt) => {
                  const selected = Math.abs(fontScale - opt.value) < 0.001;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt.key}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setFontScale(opt.value);
                        setAaOpen(false);
                      }}
                      className={cn(
                        "w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-[15px] rounded-sm hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-900",
                      )}
                    >
                      <span>{opt.label}</span>
                      <span
                        aria-hidden
                        className={cn(
                          "w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center",
                          selected ? "border-blue-600" : "border-slate-400",
                        )}
                      >
                        {selected && <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600" />}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
                {a11yExtraSections}
              </PopoverContent>
            </Popover>

            {rightTools && (
              <>
                <PipeSeparator />
                <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">{rightTools}</div>
              </>
            )}

            {timerLabelPrefix && (
              <>
                <PipeSeparator />
                <div className="pl-1 min-w-[168px]">
                  <div
                    role="timer"
                    className={cn(
                      "text-[14px] font-semibold tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap",
                      timerOverBudget ? "text-red-700" : "text-slate-900",
                    )}
                  >
                    {timerLabelPrefix}: {paddedTimer}
                  </div>
                  {timerFraction !== null && (
                    <div
                      className="mt-1 h-[5px] w-full rounded-full bg-slate-200 overflow-hidden"
                      role="progressbar"
                      aria-label="Section time remaining"
                      aria-valuemin={0}
                      aria-valuemax={100}
                      aria-valuenow={Math.round(timerFraction * 100)}
                    >
                      <div
                        className={cn("h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-1000 ease-linear", timerBarColor)}
                        style={{ width: `${timerFraction * 100}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </>
            )}

            {notesButtonVisible && onToggleNotes && (
              <>
                <PipeSeparator />
                <TooltipProvider delayDuration={150}>
                  <Tooltip>
                    <TooltipTrigger asChild>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onToggleNotes}
                        aria-label={notesOpen ? "Close notes" : "Open notes"}
                        aria-pressed={!!notesOpen}
                        className={cn(
                          "p-1.5 rounded-sm transition-colors flex items-center justify-center",
                          notesOpen
                            ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-300"
                            : "border border-transparent text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200",
                        )}
                      >
                        <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
                      </button>
                    </TooltipTrigger>
                    <TooltipContent side="bottom">
                      {notesOpen ? "Close notes" : "Open notes"}
                    </TooltipContent>
                  </Tooltip>
                </TooltipProvider>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {directions && (
        <Dialog open={directionsOpen} onOpenChange={setDirectionsOpen}>
          {/* Dressed like the player it sits over, not like the app's dark
              chrome: white, plain sans, black text, the way LawHub shows its
              directions. */}
          <DialogContent className="max-w-3xl rounded-lg border-0 bg-white p-8 font-sans text-slate-900 [&>button]:text-slate-900">
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle className="text-center text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                {directions.title}
              </DialogTitle>
              <DialogDescription className="sr-only">
                {directions.srDescription}
              </DialogDescription>
            </DialogHeader>
            <div className="space-y-4 text-[19px] leading-snug text-slate-900 [&_p]:m-0">
              {directions.body}
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

              completeLabel={isMcPractice ? 'End Drill' : undefined}
              onComplete={
                // A section's bottom bar is the navigator, which has no End —
                // so the way out of a section is this pill, as on LawHub. Every
                // other mode keeps its End in the bottom bar and hides this.
                isFullSection
                  ? () => setSubmitConfirmOpen(true)
                  : usesSmartChrome
                  ? undefined
                  : isMcPractice
                  ? () => { void handleEndBootcampPracticeDrill(); }
                  : () => handleFinishSection()
              }
              // Answer and Explanation live up here, not in the bottom bar.
              // The strip sits OUTSIDE the card that OfficialDrillFrame blurs
              // when paused, so both must fold in the paused check themselves.
              // A timed full section also keeps its old gate: revealing
              // mid-section is not an escape hatch there, it defeats the
              // exercise. (Adaptive mode never hits it — the two session modes
              // are mutually exclusive.)
              showAnswerVisible={!revealSealed}
              onShowAnswer={
                revealSealed
                  ? undefined
                  : () => toggleAnswerReveal(currentAttempted, currentRevealed)
              }
              showAnswerActive={currentRevealed}
              showAnswerDisabled={!!timer?.isPaused || (isFullSection && !currentAttempted)}
              // Explanation no longer waits on a separate reveal press: it is
              // the one control that opens the whole breakdown — correct letter,
              // every choice's explanation inline, drawer up — and pressing it
              // again collapses all of it back to an untouched question. A timed
              // section keeps the old gate, where revealing early is not a study
              // move but an escape hatch.
              onExplanation={
                revealSealed
                  ? undefined
                  : () =>
                      toggleExplanation(currentAttempted, currentRevealed, explanationOpen)
              }
              explanationActive={explanationOpen}
              explanationDisabled={
                !!timer?.isPaused || (isFullSection && !currentRevealed)
              }
              onOpenHelp={() => setHelpOpen(true)}
            />
          }
        >
          <OfficialDrillToolbar
            highlightMode={highlightMode}
            setHighlightMode={setHighlightMode}
            timerLabel={overtimeLabel ?? timer?.label ?? ""}
            timerMode={timer?.mode}
            timerProgress={timerProgress}
            searchTerm={officialSearchTerm}
            setSearchTerm={setOfficialSearchTerm}

            inlineNotes={explanationOpen ? inlineNotes : null}

            highlightMode={highlightMode}
            searchTerm={officialSearchTerm}

            onEliminate={handleEliminateAnswer}

            onHighlightClick={handleHighlightClick}
          />
          {renderVoiceCoachChip()}
          {isAdaptiveMode ? (
            <OfficialSmartDrillBar
              autoReveal={autoReveal}
              onAutoRevealChange={handleAutoRevealChange}
              primaryAction={currentAttempted || currentUnscored ? 'next' : 'submit'}
              primaryDisabled={!selectedAnswer || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onSubmit={() => {
                void commitCurrentAdaptiveAnswer({

                });
              }}
              onNext={() => setAdvanceToken(t => t + 1)}

              attemptedQids={attemptedQids}

              }
              nextDisabledReason={
                timer?.isPaused

                  ? 'Last question — use Complete Section to finish'

              }

              revealEnabled={revealSealed ? false : sectionRevealEnabled}
              onRevealEnabledChange={revealSealed ? undefined : setSectionRevealEnabled}
            />
          ) : (
            // Same chrome as the adaptive Smart Drill, but driven by the fixed
            // queue the student hand-picked from the Smart LR Drill card. Submit
            // saves and reveals in place (honoring the auto-reveal toggle); Next
            // walks the queue via the shared officialNext; Skip advances past the
            // answer gate; End finishes the section.
            <OfficialSmartDrillBar
              autoReveal={autoReveal}
              onAutoRevealChange={handleAutoRevealChange}
              primaryAction={currentAttempted || currentUnscored ? 'next' : 'submit'}
              primaryDisabled={!selectedAnswer || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onSubmit={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  const submitted = selectedAnswer;
                  await handleSubmitNonAdaptive(submitted);
                  if (autoReveal) {
                    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => new Map(prev).set(currentQuestion.qid, true));
                  }
                  // Pop the AI tutor (post-answer when revealed) on a wrong answer
                  // so the student gets a specific, back-and-forth explanation
                  // instead of the old dead "ANALYSIS VALID" placeholder.
                  const tutorLaunch = resolveTutorLaunch({
                    tutorModeEnabled: tutorMode,
                    correct: submitted === currentQuestion.correctAnswer,
                    answerRevealed: autoReveal,
                  });
                  if (tutorLaunch.shouldOpen) {
                    setAnswerLocked(true);
                    setTutorQuestionSnapshot(currentQuestion);
                    setTutorUserAnswerSnapshot(submitted);
                    setTutorChatMode(tutorLaunch.mode);
                    setTutorChatOpen(true);
                  }
                })();
              }}
              onNext={() => { void officialNext(); }}
              // A built set is a fixed list, so it walks both ways. Skip only
              // ever went forward, and only before the question was answered,
              // which left no way back to a question already worked — the one
              // thing reviewing a set is for.
              onPrev={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  await commitPendingSelection();
                  officialPrev();
                })();
              }}
              prevDisabled={session.currentIndex <= 0 || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onAdvance={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  await commitPendingSelection();
                  advanceInQueue();
                })();
              }}
              advanceDisabled={!!timer?.isPaused}
              // Submit is only ever the commit action now. Once the question is
              // answered (or deliberately revealed unscored) the Next pill is
              // what carries the student on, so a second "Next" in the primary
              // slot would just be the same control twice.
              primaryVisible={!currentAttempted && !currentUnscored}
              // A built set is a fixed, known list, so it navigates like a
              // section: every question gets an oval, and any of them can be
              // clicked. Jumping commits whatever is selected first, for the
              // same reason Previous does — leaving a question must never be
              // the thing that loses the answer on it.
              pager={
                <AnswerOvals
                  questionQueue={session.questionQueue}
                  currentIndex={session.currentIndex}
                  attemptedQids={attemptedQids}
                  jumpEnabled={!timer?.isPaused}
                  jumpDisabledReason={timer?.isPaused ? 'Paused — resume to navigate' : undefined}
                  onJumpTo={(index) => {
                    void (async () => {
                      await commitPendingSelection();
                      handleJumpTo(index);
                    })();
                  }}
                />
              }
              onEnd={() => handleNavigation('/classroom/drill')}
              disabled={!!timer?.isPaused}
            />
          )}
        </OfficialDrillFrame>
        {helpOpen && <HelpSheet open={helpOpen} onOpenChange={setHelpOpen} />}
        {renderVoiceCoachModal()}
        {renderTutorOverlay()}
      </>
    );
  }

Shown before the section on\n * its own page, and again mid-section whenever the student presses Directions.\n */\nexport function SectionDirectionsCard({\n  sectionNumber,\n  minutes,\n  questionCount,\n  directions,\n}: CardProps) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"section-directions-card\">\n      <h1 className=\"section-directions-title\">Section {sectionNumber}</h1>\n      <p className=\"section-directions-line\">\n        {minutes === null\n          ? \"Untimed section\"\n          : `Total Section Time - ${formatSectionMinutes(minutes)}`}\n      </p>\n      <p className=\"section-directions-line\">\n        {questionCount} Question{questionCount === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}\n      </p>\n      <p className=\"section-directions-body\">\n        <strong>Directions:</strong> {directions}\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface ViewProps extends CardProps {\n  /** Pre-formatted clock, e.g. \"29:22\". Empty when the section is untimed. */\n  timerLabel: string;\n  /** Paint the clock as overrun. */\n  timerOverBudget?: boolean;\n  /** Back to the question the student left. */\n  onBackToQuestions: () => void;\n}\n\n/**\n * The directions screen as LawHub shows it *during* a section: a Questions\n * button top-left where Directions used to be, the clock still counting\n * top-right, the card on the blue-grey field, and GO TO QUESTIONS underneath.\n *\n * Nothing here touches the timer. Reading the directions mid-section costs\n * exactly what it costs on test day.\n */\nexport function SectionDirectionsView({\n  timerLabel,\n  timerOverBudget,\n  onBackToQuestions,\n  ...card\n}: ViewProps) {\n  const paddedTimer = timerLabel.replace(/^(\\d):/, \"0$1:\");\n  return (\n    <div className=\"section-directions-view\" data-testid=\"section-directions-view\">\n      <div className=\"section-directions-bar\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"section-directions-questions\"\n          onClick={onBackToQuestions}\n          autoFocus\n        >\n          Questions\n        </button>\n        {timerLabel && (\n          <div className=\"section-directions-clock\">\n            <span className=\"section-directions-clock-label\">Time Remaining</span>\n            <span\n              role=\"timer\"\n              className={\n                timerOverBudget\n                  ? \"section-directions-clock-time section-directions-clock-time--over\"\n                  : \"section-directions-clock-time\"\n              }\n            >\n              {paddedTimer}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"section-directions section-directions--embedded\">\n        <SectionDirectionsCard {...card} />\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"section-directions-go\"\n          onClick={onBackToQuestions}\n        >\n          GO TO QUESTIONS\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

        if (section) {
          return {
            ...base,
            mode: 'full-section',
            config: {
              pt: section.pt,
              section: section.section,
              timer: {
                // An accommodated student carries her clock on the launch,

                mode: launch.timerMode ?? '35',
                isPaused: false,
                elapsedMs: 0,
                startedAt: now,
              },
            },

        isGuidedDrill: launch.kind === 'guided-drill',

      if (launch.kind === 'assignment') {
        spec.assignmentId = launch.assignmentId;
        spec.guidedSetId = launch.guidedSetId;

      if (launch.kind === 'guided-drill') {
        spec.guidedDurationMinutes = launch.durationMinutes;

      if (launch.kind === 'practice-set') {

## user

Tool result:
export function launchDrill(launch: DrillLaunch): string {
  const p = new URLSearchParams();
  p.set('drill', launch.kind);

  switch (launch.kind) {
    case 'smart-drill':
      break;
    case 'section':
      p.set('pt', String(launch.pt));
      p.set('section', String(launch.section));
      if (launch.timerMode) p.set('timer', launch.timerMode);
      if (launch.minutes) p.set('minutes', String(launch.minutes));
      if (launch.sealed) p.set('sealed', '1');
      if (launch.ptLeg) {
        p.set('runId', launch.ptLeg.runId);
        p.set('leg', String(launch.ptLeg.legIndex));
      }
      break;
    case 'saved-set':
      p.set('set', launch.setId);
      break;
    case 'assignment':
      p.set('assignment', launch.assignmentId);
      if (launch.guidedSetId) p.set('guidedSet', launch.guidedSetId);

# Workflow authoring reference

A workflow structures work across many agents — to be comprehensive (decompose and cover in parallel), to be confident (independent perspectives and adversarial checks before committing), or to take on scale one context can't hold (migrations, audits, broad sweeps). The script is where you encode that structure: what fans out, what verifies, what synthesizes.

When you do call it, the right move is often **hybrid**: scout inline first (list the files, find the channels, scope the diff) to discover the work-list, then call Workflow to pipeline over it. You don't need to know the shape before the *task* — only before the *orchestration step*.

Common single-phase workflows you can chain across turns:
- **Understand** — parallel readers over relevant subsystems → structured map
- **Design** — judge panel of N independent approaches → scored synthesis
- **Review** — dimensions → find → adversarially verify (the review-changes example)
- **Research** — multi-modal sweep → deep-read → synthesize
- **Migrate** — discover sites → transform each (worktree isolation) → verify

For larger work, run several in sequence — read each result before deciding the next phase. You stay in the loop; each workflow is one well-scoped fan-out.

**Ultracode.** When a system-reminder confirms ultracode is on, that opt-in is standing: author and run a workflow for every substantive task by default. The goal is the most exhaustive, correct answer you can produce — token cost is not a constraint. For multi-phase work (understand → design → implement → review), that often means several workflows in sequence — one per phase — so you stay in the loop between them. The quality patterns below (adversarial verify, multi-modal sweep, completeness critic, loop-until-dry) are the tools; pick what fits the task. Lean toward orchestrating with workflows and adversarially verifying your findings — unless the work is trivial or already verified. Solo only on conversational turns or trivial mechanical edits. When a reminder says ultracode is off, revert to the opt-in rule in the Workflow tool description.

Pass the script inline via `script` — do not Write it to a file first. Every invocation automatically persists its script to a file under the session directory and returns the path in the tool result. To iterate on a workflow, edit that file with Write/Edit and re-invoke Workflow with `{scriptPath: "<path>"}` instead of resending the full script.

Every script must begin with `export const meta = {...}`:
  export const meta = {
    name: 'find-flaky-tests',
    description: 'Find flaky tests and propose fixes',   // one-line, shown in permission dialog
    phases: [                                            // one entry per phase() call
      { title: 'Scan', detail: 'grep test logs for retries' },
      { title: 'Fix', detail: 'one agent per flaky test' },
    ],
  }
  // script body starts here — use agent()/parallel()/pipeline()/phase()/log()
  phase('Scan')
  const flaky = await agent('grep CI logs for retry markers', {schema: FLAKY_SCHEMA})
  ...

The `meta` object must be a PURE LITERAL — no variables, function calls, spreads, or template interpolation. Required fields: `name`, `description`. Optional: `whenToUse` (shown in the workflow list), `phases`. Use the SAME phase titles in meta.phases as in phase() calls — titles are matched exactly; a phase() call with no matching meta entry just gets its own progress group. Add `model` to a phase entry when that phase uses a specific model override.

Script body hooks:
- agent(prompt: string, opts?: {label?: string, phase?: string, schema?: object, model?: string, effort?: string, isolation?: 'worktree', agentType?: string}): Promise<any> — spawn a subagent. Without schema, returns its final text as a string. With schema (a JSON Schema), the subagent is forced to call a StructuredOutput tool and agent() returns the validated object — no parsing needed. Returns null if the user skips the agent mid-run or the subagent dies on a terminal API error after retries (filter with .filter(Boolean)). opts.label overrides the display label. opts.phase explicitly assigns this agent to a progress group (use this inside pipeline()/parallel() stages to avoid races on the global phase() state — same phase string → same group box). opts.model overrides the model for this agent call. Default to omitting it — the agent inherits the main-loop model (the resolved session model), which is almost always correct. Only set it when you're highly confident a different tier fits the task; when unsure, omit. opts.effort overrides the reasoning effort for this agent call ('low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'xhigh' | 'max') — omit to inherit the session effort; use 'low' for cheap mechanical stages and higher tiers only for the hardest verify/judge stages. opts.isolation: 'worktree' runs the agent in a fresh git worktree — EXPENSIVE (~200-500ms setup + disk per agent), use ONLY when agents mutate files in parallel and would otherwise conflict; the worktree is auto-removed if unchanged. opts.agentType uses a custom subagent type (e.g. 'general-purpose', 'code-reviewer') instead of the default workflow subagent — resolved from the same registry as the Agent tool; composes with schema (the custom agent's system prompt gets a StructuredOutput instruction appended).
- pipeline(items, stage1, stage2, ...): Promise<any[]> — run each item through all stages independently, NO barrier between stages. Item A can be in stage 3 while item B is still in stage 1. This is the DEFAULT for multi-stage work. Wall-clock = slowest single-item chain, not sum-of-slowest-per-stage. Every stage callback receives (prevResult, originalItem, index) — use originalItem/index in later stages to label work without threading context through stage 1's return value. A stage that throws drops that item to `null` and skips its remaining stages.
- parallel(thunks: Array<() => Promise<any>>): Promise<any[]> — run tasks concurrently. This is a BARRIER: awaits all thunks before returning. A thunk that throws (or whose agent errors) resolves to `null` in the result array — the call itself never rejects, so `.filter(Boolean)` before using the results. Use ONLY when you genuinely need all results together.
- log(message: string): void — emit a progress message to the user (shown as a narrator line above the progress tree)
- phase(title: string): void — start a new phase; subsequent agent() calls are grouped under this title in the progress display
- args: any — the value passed as Workflow's `args` input, verbatim (undefined if not provided). Pass arrays/objects as actual JSON values in the tool call, NOT as a JSON-encoded string — `args: ["a.ts", "b.ts"]`, not `args: "[\"a.ts\", ...]"` (a stringified list reaches the script as one string, so `args.filter`/`args.map` throw). Use this to parameterize named workflows — e.g. pass a research question, target path, or config object directly instead of via a side-channel file.
- budget: {total: number|null, spent(): number, remaining(): number} — the turn's token target from the user's "+500k"-style directive. `budget.total` is null if no target was set. `budget.spent()` returns output tokens spent this turn across the main loop and all workflows — the pool is shared, not per-workflow. `budget.remaining()` returns `max(0, total - spent())`, or `Infinity` if no target. The target is a HARD ceiling, not advisory: once `spent()` reaches `total`, further `agent()` calls throw. Use for dynamic loops: `while (budget.total && budget.remaining() > 50_000) { ... }`, or static scaling: `const FLEET = budget.total ? Math.floor(budget.total / 100_000) : 5`.
- workflow(nameOrRef: string | {scriptPath: string}, args?: any): Promise<any> — run another workflow inline as a sub-step and return whatever it returns. Pass a name to invoke a saved workflow (same registry as {name: "..."}), or {scriptPath} to run a script file you Wrote earlier. The child shares this run's concurrency cap, agent counter, abort signal, and token budget — its agents appear under a "▸ name" group in /workflows and its tokens count toward budget.spent(). The args param becomes the child's `args` global. Nesting is one level only: workflow() inside a child throws. Throws on unknown name / unreadable scriptPath / child syntax error; catch to handle gracefully.

Subagents are told their final text IS the return value (not a human-facing message), so they return raw data. For structured output, use the schema option — validation happens at the tool-call layer so the model retries on mismatch.
Schemas need {type: 'object', properties: {...}} at root and required ⊆ properties; unsatisfiable ones throw at agent().

Workflow agents can reach all session-connected MCP tools via ToolSearch — schemas load on demand per agent. Caveat: interactively-authenticated MCP servers (e.g. claude.ai) may be absent in headless/cron runs.

Subagents get the same CLAUDE.md files injected at start that you did (except built-in agent types that omit them, such as Explore and Plan) — don't tell them to re-read those or paste their rules into the prompt; name the specific rule a stage needs, if any.

Scripts are plain JavaScript, NOT TypeScript — type annotations (`: string[]`), interfaces, and generics fail to parse. The script body runs in an async context — use await directly. Standard JS built-ins (JSON, Math, Array, etc.) are available — EXCEPT `Date.now()`/`Math.random()`/argless `new Date()`, which throw (they would break resume); pass timestamps in via `args`, stamp results after the workflow returns, and for randomness vary the agent prompt/label by index. No filesystem or Node.js API access.

DEFAULT TO pipeline(). Only reach for a barrier (parallel between stages) when you genuinely need ALL prior-stage results together.

A barrier is correct ONLY when stage N needs cross-item context from all of stage N-1:
- Dedup/merge across the full result set before expensive downstream work
- Early-exit if the total count is zero ("0 bugs found → skip verification entirely")
- Stage N's prompt references "the other findings" for comparison

A barrier is NOT justified by:
- "I need to flatten/map/filter first" — do it inside a pipeline stage: pipeline(items, stageA, r => transform([r]).flat(), stageB)
- "The stages are conceptually separate" — that's what pipeline() models. Separate stages ≠ synchronized stages.
- "It's cleaner code" — barrier latency is real. If 5 finders run and the slowest takes 3× the fastest, a barrier wastes 2/3 of the fast finders' idle time.

Smell test: if you wrote
  const a = await parallel(...)
  const b = transform(a)        // flatten, map, filter — no cross-item dependency
  const c = await parallel(b.map(...))
that middle transform doesn't need the barrier. Rewrite as a pipeline with the transform inside a stage. When in doubt: pipeline.

Concurrent agent() calls are capped at min(16, available CPUs - 2) per workflow — excess calls queue and run as slots free up. You can still pass 100 items to parallel()/pipeline() and they all complete; only ~10 run at any moment. Total agent count across a workflow's lifetime is capped at 1000 — a runaway-loop backstop set far above any real workflow. A single parallel()/pipeline() call accepts at most 4096 items; passing more is an explicit error, not a silent truncation.

When a barrier IS correct — dedup across all findings before expensive verification:
  const all = await parallel(DIMENSIONS.map(d => () => agent(d.prompt, {schema: FINDINGS_SCHEMA})))
  const deduped = dedupeByFileAndLine(all.filter(Boolean).flatMap(r => r.findings))  // <-- genuinely needs ALL at once
  const verified = await parallel(deduped.map(f => () => agent(verifyPrompt(f), {schema: VERDICT_SCHEMA})))

Loop-until-count pattern — accumulate to a target:
  const bugs = []
  while (bugs.length < 10) {
    const result = await agent("Find bugs in this codebase.", {schema: BUGS_SCHEMA})
    bugs.push(...result.bugs)
    log(`${bugs.length}/10 found`)
  }

Loop-until-budget pattern — scale depth to the user's "+500k" directive. Guard on budget.total: with no target set, remaining() is Infinity and the loop would run straight to the 1000-agent cap.
  const bugs = []
  while (budget.total && budget.remaining() > 50_000) {
    const result = await agent("Find bugs in this codebase.", {schema: BUGS_SCHEMA})
    bugs.push(...result.bugs)
    log(`${bugs.length} found, ${Math.round(budget.remaining()/1000)}k remaining`)
  }

Composing patterns — exhaustive review (find → dedup vs seen → diverse-lens panel → loop-until-dry):
  const seen = new Set(), confirmed = []
  let dry = 0
  while (dry < 2) {                                              // loop-until-dry
    const found = (await parallel(FINDERS.map(f => () =>          // barrier: collect all finders this round
      agent(f.prompt, {phase: 'Find', schema: BUGS})))).filter(Boolean).flatMap(r => r.bugs)
    const fresh = found.filter(b => !seen.has(key(b)))           // dedup vs ALL seen — plain code, not an agent
    if (!fresh.length) { dry++; continue }
    dry = 0; fresh.forEach(b => seen.add(key(b)))
    const judged = await parallel(fresh.map(b => () =>           // every fresh bug judged concurrently...
      parallel(['correctness','security','repro'].map(lens => () =>   // ...each by 3 distinct lenses
        agent(`Judge "${b.desc}" via the ${lens} lens — real?`, {phase: 'Verify', schema: VERDICT})))
        .then(vs => ({ b, real: vs.filter(Boolean).filter(v => v.real).length >= 2 }))))
    confirmed.push(...judged.filter(v => v.real).map(v => v.b))
  }
  return confirmed
  // dedup vs `seen`, NOT `confirmed` — else judge-rejected findings reappear every round and it never converges.

Quality patterns — common shapes; pick by task and compose freely:
- Adversarial verify: spawn N independent skeptics per finding, each prompted to REFUTE. Kill if ≥majority refute. Prevents plausible-but-wrong findings from surviving.
    const votes = await parallel(Array.from({length: 3}, () => () =>
      agent(`Try to refute: ${claim}. Default to refuted=true if uncertain.`, {schema: VERDICT})))
    const survives = votes.filter(Boolean).filter(v => !v.refuted).length >= 2
- Perspective-diverse verify: when a finding can fail in more than one way, give each verifier a distinct lens (correctness, security, perf, does-it-reproduce) instead of N identical refuters — diversity catches failure modes redundancy can't.
- Judge panel: generate N independent attempts from different angles (e.g. MVP-first, risk-first, user-first), score with parallel judges, synthesize from the winner while grafting the best ideas from runners-up. Beats one-attempt-iterated when the solution space is wide.
- Loop-until-dry: for unknown-size discovery (bugs, issues, edge cases), keep spawning finders until K consecutive rounds return nothing new. Simple counters (while count < N) miss the tail.
- Multi-modal sweep: parallel agents each searching a different way (by-container, by-content, by-entity, by-time). Each is blind to what the others surface; useful when one search angle won't find everything.
- Completeness critic: a final agent that asks "what's missing — modality not run, claim unverified, source unread?" What it finds becomes the next round of work.
- No silent caps: if a workflow bounds coverage (top-N, no-retry, sampling), `log()` what was dropped — silent truncation reads as "covered everything" when it didn't.

Scale to what the user asked for. "find any bugs" → a few finders, single-vote verify. "thoroughly audit this" or "be comprehensive" → larger finder pool, 3–5 vote adversarial pass, synthesis stage. When unsure, lean toward thoroughness for research/review/audit requests and toward brevity for quick checks.

These patterns aren't exhaustive — compose novel harnesses when the task calls for it (tournament brackets, self-repair loops, staged escalation, whatever fits).

Use this tool for multi-step orchestration where control flow should be deterministic (loops, conditionals, fan-out) rather than model-driven.

## Resume

The tool result includes a runId. To resume after a pause, kill, or script edit, relaunch with Workflow({scriptPath, resumeFromRunId}) — the longest unchanged prefix of agent() calls returns cached results instantly; the first edited/new call and everything after it runs live. Same script + same args → 100% cache hit. Before diagnosing why a completed workflow returned an empty or unexpected result, Read <transcriptDir>/journal.jsonl — it records each agent's actual return value; do not assume cached results are non-empty. Date.now()/Math.random()/new Date() are unavailable in scripts (they would break this) — stamp results after the workflow returns, or pass timestamps via args. Fallback when no journal is available: Read agent-<id>.jsonl files in the transcript directory and hand-author a continuation script.

export type HighlightColor = 'yellow' | 'pink' | 'orange' | 'underline';

export interface Highlight {
  id: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  text: string;
  color: HighlightColor;
  section: 'stimulus' | 'stem';
}

export function captureTextSelection(container: HTMLElement): { start: number; end: number; text: string } | null {
  const selection = window.getSelection();
  if (!selection || selection.rangeCount === 0 || selection.isCollapsed) return null;

  const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
  if (!container.contains(range.commonAncestorContainer)) return null;

  const selectedText = selection.toString();
  if (!selectedText) return null;

  // ── Accurate offset mapping via data-para-start annotations ──────────────
  // HighlightedText annotates each <p> / dialogue-turn body with its raw-string
  // start offset. Within a single annotated segment there are no block boundaries,
  // so DOM text length == raw text length — no \n\n drift.
  const segments = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-para-start]'));

  if (segments.length > 0) {
    // Returns the character offset from the start of `seg` to `node:nodeOffset`
    const withinOffset = (seg: HTMLElement, node: Node, nodeOffset: number): number => {
      const r = document.createRange();
      r.setStart(seg, 0);
      r.setEnd(node, nodeOffset);
      return r.toString().length;
    };

    // Find the annotated segment that contains a given DOM node
    const findSeg = (node: Node): HTMLElement | null => {
      for (const seg of segments) {
        if (seg === node || seg.contains(node)) return seg;
      }
      return null;
    };

    const startSeg = findSeg(range.startContainer);
    const endSeg   = findSeg(range.endContainer);

    if (startSeg && endSeg) {
      const rawStart = parseInt(startSeg.getAttribute('data-para-start') ?? '0')
        + withinOffset(startSeg, range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
      const rawEnd   = parseInt(endSeg.getAttribute('data-para-start') ?? '0')
        + withinOffset(endSeg, range.endContainer, range.endOffset);
      return { start: rawStart, end: rawEnd, text: selectedText };
    }
  }

  // ── Fallback for containers without data-para-start ──────────────────────
  const containerRange = document.createRange();
  containerRange.selectNodeContents(container);
  const beforeRange = containerRange.cloneRange();
  beforeRange.setEnd(range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
  const start = beforeRange.toString().length;
  const end   = start + selectedText.length;
  return { start, end, text: selectedText };
}

export function replaceOverlappingHighlights(
  existing: Highlight[], 
  newHighlight: Highlight
): Highlight[] {
  const result: Highlight[] = [];
  
  for (const h of existing) {
    // No overlap - keep as is
    if (h.end <= newHighlight.start || h.start >= newHighlight.end) {
      result.push(h);
      continue;
    }
    
    // Has overlap - split and keep non-overlapping portions
    // Keep the part before the new highlight
    if (h.start < newHighlight.start) {
      const beforeText = h.text.slice(0, newHighlight.start - h.start);
      result.push({
        ...h,
        id: `${h.id}-before`,
        end: newHighlight.start,
        text: beforeText
      });
    }
    
    // Keep the part after the new highlight
    if (h.end > newHighlight.end) {
      const offset = newHighlight.end - h.start;
      const afterText = h.text.slice(offset);
      result.push({
        ...h,
        id: `${h.id}-after`,
        start: newHighlight.end,
        text: afterText
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Add the new highlight
  result.push(newHighlight);
  
  // Merge adjacent highlights of the same color
  return mergeAdjacentSameColor(result);
}

function mergeAdjacentSameColor(highlights: Highlight[]): Highlight[] {
  if (highlights.length <= 1) return highlights;
  
  const sorted = [...highlights].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const merged: Highlight[] = [];
  let current = sorted[0];
  
  for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
    const next = sorted[i];
    
    // If adjacent and same color, merge
    if (next.start === current.end && next.color === current.color && next.section === current.section) {
      current = {
        ...current,
        end: next.end,
        text: current.text + next.text,
      };
    } else {
      merged.push(current);
      current = next;
    }
  }
  merged.push(current);
  
  return merged;
}

export function mergeOverlappingHighlights(highlights: Highlight[]): Highlight[] {
  if (highlights.length <= 1) return highlights;

  const sorted = [...highlights].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const merged: Highlight[] = [];
  let current = sorted[0];

  for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
    const next = sorted[i];
    
    // If overlapping or adjacent, merge and prefer the latest color
    if (next.start <= current.end) {
      current = {
        ...next, // Use next highlight's properties (newer)
        start: Math.min(current.start, next.start),
        end: Math.max(current.end, next.end),
      };
    } else {
      merged.push(current);
      current = next;
    }
  }
  merged.push(current);

  return merged;
}

import * as React from "react";
import { DrillSplit } from '@/components/drill/DrillSplit';
import { Check, Eye, EyeOff, Flag, X } from "lucide-react";
import { HighlightedText } from "@/components/drill/HighlightedText";
import InlineChoiceExplanation, {
  type InlineChoiceNotes,
} from "@/components/drill/official/InlineChoiceExplanation";
import type { Highlight } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface Question {
  qid: string;
  qnum?: number | string;
  stimulus: string;
  questionStem: string;
  answerChoices: Record<string, string>;
}

interface Props {
  question: Question;
  displayNumber: number;
  selectedAnswer: string;
  eliminatedAnswers: Set<string>;
  isFlagged: boolean;
  answerRevealed?: boolean;
  correctAnswer?: string;
  /**
   * Per-choice explanation content, rendered inline beneath each choice once
   * the answer is revealed. Omit it entirely (section mode, or before reveal)
   * and no inline layer renders at all.
   */
  inlineNotes?: InlineChoiceNotes | null;
  highlights: Highlight[];
  highlightMode: "none" | "yellow" | "pink" | "orange" | "underline" | "erase";
  searchTerm?: string;
  fontScale?: number;
  onAnswerSelect: (key: string) => void;
  onEliminate: (key: string) => void;
  onToggleFlag: () => void;
  onTextSelection: (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>, section: "stimulus" | "stem") => void;
  onHighlightClick: (highlightId: string) => void;
  /**
   * Rendered between the stem and the choices. Blind review puts its Actual/BR
   * tabs here so it can run inside this exact pane rather than reimplementing
   * the exam screen a second time in a different visual language.
   */
  aboveChoices?: React.ReactNode;
}

function stripDataArtifacts(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/\s*\{#\d+\}\s*/g, " ")
    .replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ")
    .replace(/\n[ \t]+/g, "\n")
    .trim();
}

function cleanAnswerChoice(key: string, text: string): string {
  const escapedKey = key.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  return stripDataArtifacts(text)

                const isEliminated = eliminatedAnswers.has(key);

                // The choice they committed to, once it is known to be wrong.
                // Without this a wrong answer stayed the same neutral grey as an

                return (

                      "group relative rounded-lg border transition-all duration-200",
                      "grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-start gap-3 p-3",
                      isCorrect
                        ? "border-green-500 bg-green-50"
                        : isWrongPick
                        ? "border-red-500 bg-red-50"
                        : isSelected
                        ? "border-slate-500 bg-slate-100 shadow-sm"
                        : isEliminated
                        ? "border-transparent bg-white opacity-55 grayscale-[0.5]"
                        : "border-transparent bg-white hover:border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50",
                    )}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !isEliminated && !answerRevealed && onAnswerSelect(key)}
                      disabled={isEliminated || answerRevealed}
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border font-semibold transition-all",
                        isCorrect
                          ? "border-green-600 bg-green-500 text-white"
                          : isWrongPick
                          ? "border-red-600 bg-red-500 text-white"
                          : isSelected
                          ? "border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white"
                          : "border-transparent text-slate-950 group-hover:border-slate-300",
                      )}
                      style={choiceBadgeStyle}
                    >
                      {key}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !isEliminated && !answerRevealed && onAnswerSelect(key)}
                      disabled={isEliminated || answerRevealed}
                      className={cn(
                        "text-left leading-relaxed transition-colors",
                        isCorrect
                          ? "text-green-900"
                          : isWrongPick
                          ? "text-red-900"
                          : isSelected
                          ? "text-slate-950"
                          : "text-slate-950 group-hover:text-slate-950",
                      )}
                      style={choiceTextStyle}
                    >
                      {text}
                    </button>
                    {answerRevealed && (isCorrect || isWrongPick) ? (
                      // Colour alone must never carry the verdict (ADR 0001), and
                      // once the answer is out the cross-out control is dead
                      // anyway, so its corner becomes the icon and the label.
                      <span
                        role="img"
                        aria-label={isCorrect ? "Correct answer" : "Your answer, incorrect"}
                        title={isCorrect ? "Correct answer" : "Your answer, incorrect"}
                        className={cn(
                          "mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center",
                          isCorrect ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700",
                        )}
                      >
                        {isCorrect ? <Check size={18} /> : <X size={18} />}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(event) => {
                        event.stopPropagation();
                        if (!answerRevealed) onEliminate(key);
                      }}
                      disabled={answerRevealed}
                      // A screen reader announced this as an unlabelled button.
                      // RC's equivalent has carried both of these since it was
                      // written; this is LR catching up to it.
                      aria-label={
                        isEliminated ? `Restore choice ${key}` : `Cross out choice ${key}`
                      }
                      title={isEliminated ? "Restore" : "Cross out"}
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-0.5 p-1 transition-opacity",
                        isEliminated ? "text-slate-700" : "text-slate-400 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-slate-800",
                      )}
                    >
                      {isEliminated ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                    </button>
                    )}
                    {inline && (
                      <div className="col-start-2 col-end-4">
                        <InlineChoiceExplanation
                          letter={key}
                          isCorrect={isCorrect}
                          isSelected={isSelected}
                          content={inline}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { Highlight } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";
import { looksLikeSpeakerLabel } from "@/lib/dialogue";

interface HighlightedTextProps {
  text: string;
  highlights: Highlight[];
  onHighlightClick?: (id: string) => void;
  eraserMode?: boolean;
  /** Optional case-insensitive search term to overlay (does not mutate user highlights). */
  searchTerm?: string;
}

// ─── Single source of truth for highlight/underline styles ───────────────────
function getHighlightStyles(color: string): React.CSSProperties {
  const base: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: 'inline',
    padding: 0,
    margin: 0,
    border: 'none',
    lineHeight: 'inherit',
    letterSpacing: 'inherit',
    wordSpacing: 'inherit',
    verticalAlign: 'baseline',
    whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
    borderRadius: 0,
    WebkitBoxDecorationBreak: 'clone',
    // @ts-ignore
    boxDecorationBreak: 'clone',
  };

  if (color === 'underline') {
    return {
      ...base,
      background: 'transparent',
      backgroundImage: 'none',
      backgroundColor: 'transparent',
      mixBlendMode: 'normal',
      textDecoration: 'underline',
      textDecorationColor: '#000000',
      textDecorationThickness: '2px',
      textUnderlineOffset: '3px',
    };
  }

  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    yellow: 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.32)',
    pink: 'rgba(244, 114, 182, 0.32)',
    orange: 'rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.32)',
  };
  const bg = colors[color] ?? colors.yellow;

  return {
    ...base,
    backgroundImage: 'none',
    backgroundColor: bg,
    // Multiply keeps overlapping highlight ranges from visually doubling up.
    mixBlendMode: 'multiply',
  };
}

// ─── Search-term overlay for plain-text segments only ───────────────────────
// Wraps case-insensitive matches inside a <mark> WITHOUT touching user
// highlight spans. Returns React nodes for the given plain text slice.
function renderWithSearch(text: string, searchTerm: string | undefined, keyPrefix: string): React.ReactNode {
  if (!searchTerm || searchTerm.trim().length < 2) return text;
  const term = searchTerm.trim();
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  const lowerTerm = term.toLowerCase();
  if (!lower.includes(lowerTerm)) return text;

  const nodes: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  let cursor = 0;
  let i = 0;
  while (cursor < text.length) {
    const idx = lower.indexOf(lowerTerm, cursor);
    if (idx === -1) {
      nodes.push(text.slice(cursor));
      break;
    }
    if (idx > cursor) nodes.push(text.slice(cursor, idx));
    nodes.push(
      <mark
        key={`${keyPrefix}-s${i++}`}
        style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55)', color: 'inherit', padding: 0 }}
      >
        {text.slice(idx, idx + term.length)}
      </mark>,
    );
    cursor = idx + term.length;
  }
  return <>{nodes}</>;
}

// ─── Inline segment renderer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
function renderSegments(

  eraserMode: boolean | undefined,

Highlights are in-memory only (Map<qid, Highlight[]> in src/pages/Drill.tsx L384), never persisted. The toolbar tool state (underline/yellow/pink/orange/erase) is highlightMode. The stem already has onMouseUp={(e) => onTextSelection(e, \"stem\")} (OfficialQuestionPane ~L166). Why does Joshua experience stem highlighting as not working? Check: does HighlightedText emit data-para-start for a single-paragraph stem? Does the container passed to captureTextSelection (e.currentTarget) include the \"1.\" number span, and does that shift offsets in the fallback path? Does the <p> from HighlightedText render at all for stem text (look at how HighlightedText splits paragraphs and what it does with a string with no \\n\\n)? Is there any CSS (select-none, user-select) on the stem or its ancestors? Trace the exact code path and say what the actual bug is, with evidence. If you can't determine without a browser, say precisely which hypothesis needs a browser check.\n2. Answer choices are rendered as <button> text (OfficialQuestionPane ~L245-265). Enumerate everything required to make a drag-selection inside a choice produce a highlight and render it: DOM selectability in buttons (Chrome/Firefox/Safari behaviour for text selection inside <button>), how to keep a plain click selecting the answer while a drag with a non-collapsed selection does not, where the HighlightedText for the choice goes, how the section key should extend ('choice:A' style vs a separate field), and how eliminated/revealed/selected styling interacts with highlight spans (mix-blend-mode multiply on a green/red/grey background, opacity-55 grayscale on eliminated).\n3. Does anything else key off section === 'stimulus' | 'stem' (filters, merges, eraser logic in handleTextSelection, mergeAdjacentSameColor)? List every place the type would have to widen.\n4. The classic layout in Drill.tsx (~L4570-4630): does it render choices? How? What is needed there?\nAlso list existing tests: src/components/drill/official/OfficialQuestionPane.test.tsx and any highlightUtils/HighlightedText tests, and which assertions would be affected.`, { label: 'read:official-pane', phase: 'Understand', schema: FINDINGS }),\n\n  () => agent(`${common}\n\nYour slice: every OTHER LR question surface that renders a stem and answer choices with (or without) highlighting. Ignore src/references/ (frozen reference copies).\n\nFor each surface: which route/mode uses it (is it a *drilling* surface a student answers questions in, or a review/lesson surface?), whether it reuses OfficialQuestionPane or has its own rendering, whether it has its own highlight state and selection handler, and exactly what it would take to support stem + choice highlighting there. Say explicitly which surfaces are out of scope for \"highlight during drilling\" (e.g. a read-only lesson card) and why. Also identify whether any of these pass highlights/onTextSelection through to OfficialQuestionPane, so that a change to the pane's props signature would need a matching change there. Report line numbers.`, { label: 'read:other-lr-surfaces', phase: 'Understand', schema: FINDINGS }),\n\n  () => agent(`${common}\n\nYour slice: the Reading Comprehension drill, which has its own annotation system. Read: src/features/rc/hooks/useRcHighlights.ts (note target: 'passage' | RcLetter), src/features/rc/components/RcPassagePane.tsx, RcQuestionPane.tsx, RcChoiceRow.tsx, RcExamPane.tsx, RcChrome.tsx, and src/features/rc/pages/RcPlayer.tsx around L240-260 and L660-780. Also supabase/migrations/20260806000000_rc_drilling.sql for the rc_annotations table shape.\n\nAnswer: (1) Can a student, in the RC player today, highlight or underline text inside the question stem? inside an answer choice? Trace whether RcQuestionPane/RcChoiceRow ever call highlights.add with a letter target, and whether they render existing letter-target annotations. (2) If not, what exactly is missing (selection capture, rendering, the stem having no target value in the store, DB column constraints), and how much of RcPassagePane's selection logic is reusable. (3) Is the RC store's target model persisted per passage such that a stem target would need a new value? Report line numbers. Keep it factual; if choice highlighting already works in RC, say so plainly with the evidence.`, { label: 'read:rc-player', phase: 'Understand', schema: FINDINGS }),\n])\n\nreturn { official, others, rc }"}

      </span>
    );
    cursor = h.end;
  }

  if (cursor < text.length) {
    const slice = text.slice(cursor);
    nodes.push(<span key={`${prefix}-tend`}>{renderWithSearch(slice, searchTerm, `${prefix}-tend`)}</span>);
  }

  return <>{nodes}</>;
}

// ─── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function HighlightedText({
  text,
  highlights,
  onHighlightClick,
  eraserMode,
  searchTerm,
}: HighlightedTextProps) {
  const speakerPattern = /^([A-Z][A-Za-z’' -]{1,80}):\s+/;
  const turnSplitPattern = /(?=^[A-Z][A-Za-z’' -]{1,80}:\s)/gm;
  const rawSegments = text.split(turnSplitPattern).filter(s => s.trim());

  const dialogueTurns = rawSegments
    .map(seg => {
      const match = seg.match(speakerPattern);
      if (match && looksLikeSpeakerLabel(match[1])) {
        return { speaker: match[1], text: seg.slice(match[0].length).trim() };
      }
      return null;
    })
    .filter(Boolean);

  const isDialogue =
    dialogueTurns.length >= 2 &&
    dialogueTurns.length === rawSegments.length &&
    dialogueTurns.every(t => t !== null);

  if (isDialogue) {
    let currentOffset = 0;
    const turnRanges = dialogueTurns.map(turn => {
      const speakerPrefix = `${turn!.speaker}: `;
      const turnStart = text.indexOf(speakerPrefix, currentOffset);
      const start = turnStart + speakerPrefix.length;
      const end = start + turn!.text.length;
      currentOffset = end;
      return { turn: turn!, start, end };
    });

    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {turnRanges.map((tr, idx) => {
          const turnHighlights = highlights
            .filter(h => h.start < tr.end && h.end > tr.start)
            .map(h => ({
              ...h,
              start: Math.max(0, h.start - tr.start),
              end: Math.min(tr.turn.text.length, h.end - tr.start),
            }));

          return (
            <div key={idx} className="leading-relaxed" style={{ lineHeight: 1.7 }}>
              <span className="font-bold">{tr.turn.speaker}:</span>{' '}
              <span className="inline" data-para-start={tr.start}>
                {renderSegments(tr.turn.text, turnHighlights, `d${idx}`, eraserMode, onHighlightClick, searchTerm)}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const paragraphs = text.split('\n\n');

  let currentOffset = 0;
  const paragraphRanges = paragraphs.map(para => {
    const start = currentOffset;
    const end = currentOffset + para.length;
    currentOffset = end + 2;
    return { start, end, text: para };
  });

  return (
    <>
      {paragraphRanges.map((pr, idx) => {
        const paraHighlights = highlights
          .filter(h => h.start < pr.end && h.end > pr.start)
          .map(h => ({
            ...h,
            start: Math.max(0, h.start - pr.start),
            end: Math.min(pr.text.length, h.end - pr.start),
          }));

        return (
          <p key={idx} data-para-start={pr.start} style={{ margin: '0 0 12px', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {renderSegments(pr.text, paraHighlights, `p${idx}`, eraserMode, onHighlightClick, searchTerm)}
          </p>
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
}
